Method: 作者开发了一个由大语言模型驱动的工业机械臂，能够采用高与低两种自我披露策略参与闲聊，并以50名参与者开展用户实验，比较两种策略下的人机互动效果。

Result: 结果显示，机器人采用低自我披露时，参与者倾向于更多自我披露，且团队合作与协调感更强，特别是在有机器人协作经验的用户中更为显著。

Conclusion: 机器人自我披露越多并不必然增进亲密感或信息互换，实际设计中需考虑参与者的先验人机协作经验等其他因素。

Abstract: People naturally chat while collaborating and share personal information (i.e., self-disclose) to build rapport and maintain social connections. As robots are increasingly developed to work with people, the effective use of these social behaviors to enhance engagement and support teamwork becomes ever more important. While prior work has shown that robot-initiated small talk can benefit human-robot collaboration, less is known about how best to design such small talk. In this work, we explore how self-disclosure may be designed to support small talk within a human-robot team---especially when the robot is an industrial manipulator that lacks anthropomorphic cues and performs physical work. We first developed an LLM-driven manipulator capable of partaking in small talk, adopting either a low-disclosure or high-disclosure strategy. We then conducted a user study (N = 50) to investigate how self-disclosure in small talk influences human-robot dynamics. Unexpectedly, participants disclosed more in the low-disclosure condition and reported stronger teaming and coordination than those in the high-disclosure condition. This effect was more pronounced among users with prior experience teaming with robots. These results suggest that increasing robot self-disclosure does not necessarily foster rapport, social connection, or reciprocal disclosure; other factors, such as prior HRI experience, should be considered.

</details>


### [147] [Picking Bins Empty: A Hierarchical Hybrid Approach with Online Self-Learning of Grasp Points for Reliable Industrial Bin-Picking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28175)
*Florian Töper,Samarth Kishor Yelvande,Jan Niklas Ewertz,Rudolph Triebel,Peter Ohlhausen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层混合方法，将基于模型和无模型的抓取方法结合，极大提升了工业自动分拣精度，实现了100%的料箱清空率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的料箱分拣方法要么依赖于基于模型的高精度算法，但容易受遮挡或感知失败影响而进入死锁；要么使用免模型方法，虽通用但不够可靠，难以满足生产需求。此外，基于模型的新零件抓取 usually 需要繁琐的抓取点微调，增加了人工成本。因此，亟需一种既能保证性能又减少人工介入的新方法。

Method: 本文提出了四层分层混合框架：以鲁棒的基于模型流程为主干，辅以无模型的探索代理来解决死锁和发现新的抓取点；利用夹爪反馈与Wilson区间实现在线自学习，对候选抓点自主评分排序，显著减少人工精调需求。

Result: 在三个汽车零件上的实验表明，该混合方法相较于纯无模型基线大幅提升了抓取成功率，并将纯基于模型法的箱体清空率从50.9%提升到100%。

Conclusion: 该方法有效结合了两类技术优点，显著提升工业分拣的自动化水平，实现了真正意义上的无需人工干预的高效分拣，为工业自动化发展迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Bin-picking is a cornerstone of modern manufacturing, yet achieving complete bin clearance without manual intervention remains a critical challenge. While model-based methods provide high precision, they frequently suffer from deadlocks when predefined grasps are occluded or perception fails. Labor-intensive fine-tuning of grasp points is commonly required to reach a satisfactory performance for new parts. Model-free algorithms offer a more flexible alternative with "out-of-the-box" versatility but lack the reliability and repeatability required for production. Unlike existing work, which treats the two techniques in isolation, we propose a fourtiered hierarchical hybrid approach to combine the best of both worlds. A model-based pipeline serves as a robust backbone, while a model-free "exploration agent" resolves deadlock situations and discovers new grasp points. This is supported by an online self-learning mechanism that uses gripper-stroke feedback and Wilson score intervals to autonomously rank grasp candidates, reducing manual commissioning effort. Validation on three automotive parts demonstrates that our method significantly outperforms a model-free baseline in grasp success rate while improving the bin clearance rate of the model-based baseline from 50.9% to 100% across all experiments. This transition to full bin clearance marks a significant step towards truly autonomous, intervention-free industrial operation.

</details>


### [148] [PAMoR: Parameterized Affective Motion Generation in Real Time for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28213)
*Yan Pan,Lingfan Bao,Tianhu Peng,Chengxu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将情感转化为机器人动作生成可控参数的新方法，并验证了其在情感和动作可编辑性的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去用于虚拟人或机器人情感动作生成的方法多通过参考片段或情感词来体现风格，缺乏可量化、参数化的控制方式。这阻碍了机器人动作中情感表达的精确控制。

Method: 提出PAMoR方法：直接在机器人运动学数据上计算情感的valence-arousal（V-A）坐标，基于姿态扩展和运动能量的定量测量。不需人工标注。动作先验和双情感先验共同作用，生成过程中分别控制动作内容与表达风格，在Unitree G1机器人上实现全身动作实时自回归生成。

Result: 生成的动作能在V-A全范围内准确追踪指令情感参数，且文本-动作的拟合度与无情感基线相当。感知实验中，受试者对所生成动作识别情感概率为0.38，高于基线值，接近真人表演（0.44）。

Conclusion: PAMoR首次实现了基于测量维度（V-A）对机器人动作情感进行可编辑控制，兼顾动作完成度与情感表达，向机器人在社会场景下自然互动迈进一步。

Abstract: People read a humanoid robot's motion in social settings not only for the action performed but for the affect conveyed. Motion carrying that affect has so far been generated for human avatars, where style is taken from a reference clip or an emotion word, neither of which can be quantitatively parameterized. We present PAMoR, which turns affect into a measured control parameter: a valence-arousal (V-A) coordinate computed natively on robot kinematics. It is obtained in closed form from postural expansion and movement energy, and these measurements serve directly as generation conditions, with no human annotation. An action prior and two affect priors, trained in a shared latent space, are composed at each denoising step: the action prior fixes what is performed, the affect priors modulate how. Whole-body motion rolls out autoregressively on a 29-DoF Unitree G1 in real time, with action and affect both editable. Generated motion tracks the commanded V-A over its full range while text-to-motion fidelity still matches text-only baselines. In a perceptual study, raters identify the commanded emotion on 0.38 of trials, above both baselines and approaching the 0.44 reported for acted human bodies.

</details>


### [149] [Probabilistic Multi-Robot Gas Source Localization with Uncalibrated Sensors: A Distributed Estimation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28214)
*Wanting Jin,Marc Zoel Arias Mitjà,Alcherio Martinoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布式概率框架，实现了多机器人系统在传感器异质且未校准条件下的无校准源定位。方法实现高效且准确的信息融合，并在仿真中显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人环境监测任务中，机器人常用的传感器往往存在非线性和响应不一致等问题，导致信息难以可靠融合。如何在无需事先传感器校准的条件下，实现环境状态的准确估计，是亟需解决的难题。

Method: 提出每台机器人独立利用基于排序的特征计算产生对观测演化的本地置信度，通过专家乘积法则进行全局置信融合；同时加入信息区分配与路径规划机制，提升团队协调效率。

Result: 高保真仿真验证显示，该方法在传感器异质性较强时，定位准确率明显优于基于传统测量汇总的基准方法，展现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提无校准分布式融合框架有效提升了异构机器人团队的源定位表现，证明了无校准感知表示在分布式系统中的可扩展性，为异构传感器下的其他估计任务开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Estimating environmental states with multi-robot systems becomes particularly challenging when robots are equipped with uncalibrated and therefore heterogeneous sensors, whose nonlinear and inconsistent responses prevent reliable information fusion. In this paper, we propose a distributed probabilistic framework for source localization tasks that enables calibration-free estimation in the presence of sensor heterogeneity. The key idea is that each robot independently estimates a local belief using a rank-based feature that captures the relative evolution of observations and is invariant to sensor scaling and nonlinearities. These local beliefs are then fused through a product of experts formulation to obtain a consistent global estimate across the team. To further improve the efficiency of team coordination, we introduce an informative region allocation and path planning strategy that reduces redundant exploration while balancing exploration and exploitation. We validate the proposed framework using high-fidelity simulations with realistic gas sensor models. Results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms a benchmark method based on standard measurement aggregation, achieving reliable source localization accuracy despite strong sensor heterogeneity. More broadly, this work demonstrates how calibration-free sensing representations can be effectively extended to distributed robotic systems, paving the way for their application to other estimation tasks involving heterogeneous sensors.

</details>


### [150] [Training-free Suction Grasp Detection for Deformed Aseptic Cartons Using Vision-Language Models and Geometric Surface Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28246)
*Marin Maletic,Goran Vasiljevic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的机器人吸取分拣系统，能够在无需学习的情况下高效分拣变形的无菌饮料纸盒，综合采用视觉语言模型、实例分割和几何评分方法选择合适吸取点，实际场景中分拣准确率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可回收垃圾的机器人分拣因为目标物体易变形且几何结构多变而非常困难。现实需求却强烈，例如回收站需要高效、可靠的自动分拣系统，尤其是各种状态的饮料纸盒。因此，开发适应变形物体的分拣方法，有助于提升分拣自动化和回收效率。

Method: 该系统将目标检测与吸取点选择解耦。首先，利用开源视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）通过文本提示检测出纸盒；随后用SAM2进一步获得实例掩码；最后，采用几何打分法（包括k近邻PCA、Sobel叉积、RANSAC平面拟合三种方法）通过表面平整度和法线一致性选择最优吸取点。

Result: 在真实机器人和三种不同变形程度、35个复杂场景中测试，单目标吸取成功率达88.2%，在杂乱环境下端到端回收成功率达到72.6%。

Conclusion: 所提系统无需训练，能鲁棒地分拣变形饮料纸盒，对开放场景和复杂物体形变具备良好适应性，有助于实际垃圾分拣机器人部署。

Abstract: Robotic sorting of recyclable waste is challenging due to the deformable and geometrically inconsistent nature of target objects. We present a training-free suction grasping system for sorting deformed aseptic beverage cartons, decoupling target identification from grasp-point selection. An open-vocabulary vision-language model detects cartons from a text prompt, SAM2 refines each detection into an instance mask, and a geometric scoring method selects the suction point by combining surface flatness with normal alignment. Three geometric methods are compared: k-nearest-neighbour PCA, Sobel cross-product, and RANSAC plane fitting. Evaluated on a real robot across three deformation levels and 35 cluttered scenes, single-object grasp success reaches 88.2% and end-to-end retrieval in clutter is 72.6%.

</details>


### [151] [CoCoBench: A Cooperative Coordination Benchmark for Embodied Multi-Agent Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28266)
*Yang Chen,Ye-Xin Xie,Lirong Che,Danyang Peng,Yuzhe Yang,Peiwen Lin,Xu Cao,Chuang Wang,Lei Yuan,Jian Su,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoCoBench，用于细粒度评估多模态大语言模型驱动的多智能体协作能力，弥补现有基准在多智能体细节分析不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体基准多只关注整体任务完成率，缺乏对具体协作失败情况如重复劳动、顺序违约、资源争用等的细致检测。这使研究者难以发现和改进智能体协作瓶颈。

Method: 作者设计了CoCoBench基准，包含897个经oracle验证的家庭任务案例，围绕任务分配、顺序、互斥、交接等四种核心协作结构，为每种结构提供专门评分，并对11种主流多模态大语言模型在不同协作模式与观测输入下的表现进行评测。

Result: 实验表明，模型的协作能力对不同协作结构高度敏感，总体成绩好不代表在所有类型任务中表现均衡，各模型在特定协作点上能力差异显著。

Conclusion: 评测结果揭示了现有模型在多智能体细分协作能力上的不足，指出今后可针对不同协作类型定向改进模型架构，以提升多智能体系统的整体与细粒度协作表现。

Abstract: Agent systems powered by multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced rapidly in recent years, yet existing embodied-agent benchmarks still lack fine-grained diagnostics for multi-agent coordination. Most benchmarks either focus on single-agent task completion or summarize multi-agent behavior with overall task success rates, which can obscure coordination failures such as duplicated work, violations of ordering constraints, resource contention, and desynchronized handoffs. In this paper, we introduce CoCoBench, a construct-level benchmark for evaluating multi-agent embodied coordination in executable household tasks. CoCoBench contains 897 oracle-validated instances organized around four recurring coordination constructs: task allocation, sequential ordering, mutual exclusion, and handoff coordination. In addition to task success rate, CoCoBench provides construct-level scores that measure whether agents coordinate effectively. We evaluate 11 leading MLLMs across different coordination modes, observation inputs, and numbers of agents. The results show that coordination ability is highly construct-specific: strong overall performance does not imply balanced competence across different coordination types. These findings point to new directions for designing targeted model architectures and improving multi-agent coordination ability.

</details>


### [152] [Spatial-Semantic Reasoning using Large Language Models for Efficient UAV Search Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28270)
*Marin Maletic,Marijana Peti,Tamara Petrovic,Stjepan Bogdan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合大语言模型（LLM）、语义推理和实时感知的无人机智能导航系统，实现了高效的目标导航任务。该系统在真实与模拟环境下都展示了提高任务效率与准确性的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前目标导航任务存在对离线推理、模拟器限制等问题，难以满足实时、高效的无人机导航需求。需要能支持自然语言指令、动态语义推理的系统来提升实际应用价值。

Method: 本方法通过引入大语言模型理解自然语言指令，并动态结合检测到的目标和空间关系，推断最优的搜索区。同时结合实时目标检测、三维空间建图和多项式轨迹插值，实现平滑、可行的无人机路径规划。

Result: 实验结果显示，在真实与模拟环境下，该方法能有效缩短任务用时，同时保持高搜索准确率，验证了系统在实际应用场景中的优越性。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的语义推理显著提升了无人机在ObjectNav任务中的时间效率和准确性，为无人机实时高效地执行复杂任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present a real-time semantic navigation framework for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) focused on improving time efficiency in the Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav) task. Central to our approach is a Large Language Model (LLM) that interprets user-provided natural language instructions and performs semantic reasoning over detected objects and spatial context to prioritize high-probability search regions. The system combines real-time object detection, 3D spatial mapping, and polynomial spline interpolation for smooth and feasible UAV trajectory planning. Unlike prior methods that rely on offline reasoning or simulator-constrained action spaces, our framework can operate in real time, continuously updating semantic relevance based on new observations. Experiments in both simulated and real-world settings demonstrate reductions in mission duration while maintaining high search accuracy, underscoring the effectiveness of LLM-guided reasoning for time- efficient UAV-based ObjectNav.

</details>


### [153] [STEGNav: Spatio-Temporal Event Graph Reasoning for Multimodal Lifelong Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28279)
*Yang Chen,Zhenyu Huang,Wenbo Fu,Danyang Peng,Shi-Yu Tian,Kun-Yang Yu,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的导航方法STEGNav，实现对多模态任务的自主探索，并通过时空事件图提升导航效率和体验复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖语义场景图，但难以有效区分相似实例、联合表示目标与探索前沿、以及利用导航记忆与轨迹经验，因而影响了多模态终身导航任务的性能。

Method: 提出无训练的Spatio-Temporal Event Graph Navigation（STEGNav）框架，将传统场景图扩展为时空事件图，分别在空间轴实现条件化实例定位和探索前沿联合表示，在时间轴通过轨迹感知的双窗口记忆管理导航事件和跨任务经验。最终通过视觉语言模型（VLM）作为导航主体，基于事件图决策下一个目标或探索点。

Result: 在GOAT-Bench上达到66.3%的成功率（SR）和39.7的SPL；在HM3Dv1和HM3Dv2指标上的SR分别为64.0%和69.4%。消融实验和错误分析验证了空间轴和时间轴的互补优势。

Conclusion: STEGNav利用事件驱动的时空表示，显著提升了多模态导航的可靠性和跨任务经验的复用潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal lifelong navigation requires an agent to autonomously explore unseen environments while sequentially completing navigation tasks specified by object categories, language descriptions, or reference images. Existing methods primarily accomplish these tasks by constructing state-centric semantic scene graphs. By treating scene graphs as persistent repositories of semantic observations, these methods struggle to distinguish similar instances, jointly represent semantic targets and exploration frontiers, and effectively exploit navigation memory and trajectory experience. To address these limitations, we propose Spatio-Temporal Event Graph Navigation (STEGNav), a training-free framework that extends conventional scene graphs into spatio-temporal event graphs along complementary spatial and temporal axes. The spatial axis performs query-conditioned instance grounding and jointly represents semantic targets and occupancy-aware exploration frontiers characterized by reachability, path cost, and exploration utility. The temporal axis employs trajectory-aware dual-window memory to retain recent decision--trajectory events and verified cross-subtask navigation outcomes. A VLM-based navigation agent reasons over the resulting spatio-temporal event graph and selects either a target instance or an exploration frontier as its next navigation goal. STEGNav achieves 66.3% SR and 39.7 SPL on GOAT-Bench, as well as SR scores of 64.0% and 69.4% on HM3Dv1 and HM3Dv2, respectively. Ablation studies and error analyses validate the complementary effects of the two axes, demonstrating that event-driven spatio-temporal representations improve navigation reliability and cross-subtask experience reuse.

</details>


### [154] [MaCoPlanner: LLM-Assisted Manual-Compiled Task Planning with Proactive Safety Verification for Robotic Industrial Panel Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28300)
*Guipeng Xin,Jiahe Xua,Mohammad Deghat,Chenhui Wan,Jie Liu,Youmin Hu,Zhongxu Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MaCoPlanner是一种基于知识的任务规划系统，可自动理解设备手册并转化为可执行操作，显著提升机器人操作工业面板的安全合规性和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人自动执行工业控制面板操作面临操作安全、步骤合规与复杂设备状态约束等实际难题，而现有方案难以充分整合分散于异构手册中的知识，易违规范、出错。

Method: 提出MaCoPlanner框架，将设备手册知识结构化为中间表示，结合任务与状态证据进行任务规划。在执行前，对候选计划进行符号化回放，检测流程和状态约束违例，并定位修复；不可修复则拒绝执行。执行接口则将符号动作映射为物理控制并动态更新设备状态。

Result: MaCoPlanner实现2.7%的违例终态率，26.3%的运行在耗尽修正预算后被拒绝。相比直接依赖原始手册的方案，二级任务成功率从62.8%升至84.4%，三级任务从25.9%升至43.2%。

Conclusion: MaCoPlanner有效整合手册知识，极大提升机器人工业面板操作的安全、合规与成功率，且在模拟器中验证了其一体化执行的可行性，但尚未实现实际工业部署。

Abstract: Robotic industrial panel operation requires not only accurate control localization but also compliance with operating procedures, safety rules, and device-state constraints distributed across heterogeneous manuals. This study presents MaCoPlanner, a task-planning framework built on knowledge compiled from equipment manuals that converts equipment manuals into a typed intermediate representation, retrieves task- and state-relevant evidence, and uses it to support plan generation. Before actuation, candidate plans are symbolically rolled out and checked against procedural and state-transition constraints; detected violations are localized and returned for targeted repair, while unresolved plans are rejected. A separate execution interface grounds verified symbolic actions to physical controls and updates the device state. Under an independent evaluation oracle, MaCoPlanner achieves a final violation rate of 2.7%, and 26.3% of the runs in the repair analysis are rejected after exhausting the refinement budget. Compared with Raw-Manual, task success increases from 62.8% to 84.4% on Level-2 tasks and from 25.9% to 43.2% on Level-3 tasks. Experiments on a controller-panel simulator without an attached industrial load further demonstrate integrated execution feasibility under representative interaction conditions, without claiming industrial deployment readiness.

</details>


### [155] [PanelShield: Verifiable Closed-Loop Safe Planning for Robotic Industrial Panel Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28305)
*Guipeng Xin,Jiahe Xu,Chenhui Wan,Jie Liu,Youmin Hu,Zhongxu Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了PanelShield框架，用于工业面板操作的安全计划，能够检测并修复步骤违规，显著提升安全性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业面板操作任务涉及大量知识且具有高度安全风险，常规大模型规划方法缺乏明确的违规检测与修复机制，难以满足严格的操作手册和安全规范要求。

Method: 提出PanelShield：从操作手册中提取任务相关证据，生成参数化基础动作序列，并采用LTL（线性时序逻辑）与安全有限状态机（FSM）双重形式化验证，确保操作序列跨步骤的时序正确性和局部转移合法性。发生违规时，能定位并输出最早违规步骤及原因，便于有针对性的修复和再次验证。

Result: 在三个工业面板的多级、长时规划基准测试中，PanelShield在仿真和真实机器人实验中，将违规率降至2.7%，总体规划延迟仅4.1秒，性能优于仅依赖大模型的规划基线。

Conclusion: PanelShield为工业面板机器人操作提供了一种可验证、兼顾灵活性和安全性的闭环规划方法，兼顾可追溯性，具备端到端落地可行性。

Abstract: Industrial panel operation is knowledge-intensive and safety-critical. Beyond control recognition and action generation, execution must satisfy constraints in operation manuals and safety regulations. While foundation-model-based planners show strong semantic capability, they typically lack computable, localizable, and reproducible mechanisms for violation detection and repair. To address this, we propose PanelShield, a verifiable closed-loop safety planning framework for manual-guided industrial panel operation. The framework generates parameterized action primitive sequences from task-relevant manual evidence and applies dual formal verification with LTL and a Safety FSM to enforce cross-step temporal correctness and local transition legality. When violations occur, it outputs a structured counterexample with the earliest violating step and cause, enabling targeted repair and re-verification. We build a multi-level long-horizon planning benchmark covering three representative industrial device panels, and evaluate the framework in simulation and real-world robotic experiments. Results show that PanelShield improves complex safety-constrained task performance over foundation-model-only planning baselines while reducing the violation rate to 2.7%, with 4.1 s total latency. Real-world experiments demonstrate end-toend feasibility. Overall, PanelShield offers a verifiable approach to robotic panel operation that balances flexibility, safety, and auditability.

</details>


### [156] [Cooperative Risk-Aware Exploration in Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Systems Using Algorithmic Altruism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28409)
*Brooks A. Butler,Jair Certório,João P. Hespanha,Magnus Egerstedt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生态启发利他行为的博弈论多机器人协作探索方法，能在保障团队总体效能的同时合理分配探索风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人系统在危险环境勘探中多关注信息获取地点选择，较少关注如何根据机器人异质性分配风险。本研究旨在提出兼顾信息效率与风险分担的协作机制。

Method: 引入博弈论结构，利用个体价值参数与相关性权重（基于Hamilton's rule）建立社会纳什均衡，每个机器人在有限视域内选择轨迹以最大化信息增益并最小化重复及风险。提出基于梯度的动态规划算法进行实时轨迹优化，并在仿真和实际硬件上验证。

Result: 仿真与实验证明，该利他式规划减少了重复探索，提高了机器人的分散性，并根据价值合理分配风险，同时保持了地图覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了多机器人在危险环境下的协调探索能力，实现了风险与资源的优化分配，适用于实际应用场景。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems are well-positioned for exploration in hazardous environments, but effective deployment requires deciding not only where robots should gather information, but also how risk should be distributed across heterogeneous team members. This paper develops a game-theoretic framework for cooperative risk-aware exploration based on ecologically inspired altruistic behavior. Each robot selects a finite-horizon trajectory to maximize information gain while penalizing redundant exploration and expected hazard exposure. Heterogeneity is introduced through agent-specific value parameters for encoding altruistic coupling, which is modeled through relatedness weights inspired by Hamilton's rule. We introduce a game-theoretic structure for trajectory planning that defines a Social Nash Equilibrium, which modifies the utility of agent actions according to agent relatedness. This utility shaping causes agents to internalize the effect of their trajectory choices on teammates, encouraging lower-valued robots to accept risk when doing so benefits higher-valued agents and improves team performance. We define an exploration utility for agents that rewards area coverage and uncertainty reduction, while also penalizing redundancy and risk, enabling projected gradient-based waypoint optimization in a receding-horizon planner. Simulations show that altruistic planning reduces redundant exploration, improves inter-robot separation, and reallocates risk according to agent value while maintaining comparable map coverage. We further demonstrate the approach in hardware experiments, where planned waypoints are tracked by wheeled robots using single-integrator controllers and barrier certificates.

</details>


### [157] [Linear Temporal Logic Translation via Human-Inspired Self-Constrained Reasoning for Robot Task Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28435)
*Haofei Hou,Fanxu Meng,Shunyi Zhao,Kairui Yang,Mengchen Cai,Lecheng Ruan,Qining Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的自约束推理（SCR）框架，用于将自然语言任务指令高效、准确地翻译为机器人可验证的形式化规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通常使用自然语言表达复杂、时序性的机器人任务，但自然语言含糊、信息不足且依赖上下文，导致难以直接用于机器人的自动执行。需要将其转化为如LTL一类的形式化任务描述以保证机器人的可验证性与安全性。

Method: 提出了自约束推理（SCR）框架，将结构性知识直接融入模型决策流程，采用结构约束表示加层次化决策策略，引导大模型在形式化约束空间内进行推理，而不是事后施加外部约束。

Result: 实验表明SCR方法比现有的大语言模型翻译器在满足领域约束和泛化能力方面表现更优。

Conclusion: SCR为人类意图到机器人可验证规范的翻译提供了一种兼具效果和可解释性的解决方案，提升了自动化机器人任务推理的安全性和通用性。

Abstract: Many robotic tasks are temporally extended and demand precise specifications of subgoals, constraints, and their temporal ordering. Yet human operators typically communicate such tasks in natural language, which is inherently ambiguous, underspecified, and context dependent. Translating human instructions into formal task specifications, such as Linear Temporal Logic (LTL), is therefore essential for verifiable and safe robotic execution. Existing LLM-based translators attempt to bridge this gap through open-ended reasoning or post-hoc constraint enforcement, but the former may violate domain constraints, whereas the latter can disrupt the reasoning needed for novel instructions. This paper proposes Self-Constrained Reasoning (SCR), a framework that mitigates this trade-off by internalizing structural knowledge into the model's decision-making process rather than imposing it as an external filter. By combining a structural constraint representation with a hierarchical decision-making formulation, SCR guides reasoning within a formally grounded space while preserving adaptability to unseen instructions. Experiments show that SCR improves both domain-constraint satisfaction and generalization, providing an effective and interpretable approach for translating human intent into verifiable specifications for robotic execution.

</details>


### [158] [ChainSplat: A Physics-Inspired Screw-Theoretic Model for Learning Deformable Linear Object Dynamics from Multi-View RGB Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28570)
*Seungyeon Kim,Noémie Jaquier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ChainSplat，一种无需深度信息，仅基于多视角RGB视频，同时学习柔性线状物体（如绳索、电缆）三维几何、运动学和动力学的新框架，可高效实现高精度动态预测、三维重建及渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有方法依赖多阶段流程和深度输入，且高维状态表示导致模型控制计算开销大、易出错等问题，旨在提升柔性物体在动态交互中的三维理解与操控效率和准确性。

Method: 将柔性线状物体建模为由铰接关节串联的刚性链式结构，采用螺旋理论进行解析建模；同时结合高斯分布渲染技术，从多视角RGB视频中联合学习物体的动力学、运动学相关三维形状以及外观特性，实现端到端的一体化过程。

Result: 在真实环境实验中，ChainSplat在动力学预测、三维重建和RGB渲染等方面显著超越现有方法，能实现高精度、实时的状态与受力估计，并提升模型控制与路径优化能力。

Conclusion: ChainSplat为柔性线状物体的三维动态理解和机器人实际操控提供了高效、精准的解决方案，展示了优越的实用价值，并为相关领域方法带来新方向。

Abstract: Identifying the underlying dynamics and 3D geometry of deformable linear objects (DLOs), such as cables, ropes, and hoses, is essential for accurate robotic manipulation, but remains challenging due to their high-dimensional configuration spaces and diverse behaviors arising from varying material properties. Existing methods often rely on multi-stage pipelines and auxiliary depth inputs, which are prone to errors under dynamic interactions, while their high-dimensional state representations make model-based control computationally expensive. In this paper, we introduce ChainSplat, a physics-inspired framework that jointly learns the 3D geometry, appearance, kinematics, and dynamics of DLOs solely from multi-view RGB videos. ChainSplat represents a DLO as an open-chain structure of rigid links connected by revolute joints, yielding an analytic, screw-theoretic model with a compact state representation parameterized by joint configurations. By integrating this formulation with Gaussian splatting, ChainSplat jointly recovers DLO dynamics, kinematics-aware 3D geometry, and appearance, while enabling high-fidelity RGB rendering from arbitrary states. Through real-world experiments, we demonstrate that ChainSplat achieves state-of-the-art performance in dynamics predictions, 3D geometry reconstruction, and RGB rendering across dynamic interactions. ChainSplat further enables real-time state and force estimation, as well as accurate model-based trajectory optimization, highlighting its practical utility for real-world robotic manipulation of DLOs. Accompanying source code and video are available at: https://chainsplat.github.io.

</details>


### [159] [Aero Hand Open: A Simulation-Ready Tendon-Driven Hand for Dexterous Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28578)
*Nan Wang,Mohit Yadav,Jonathan Wulff,Aidan Rosenbaum,Kezhou Chen,Yuvan Sharma,Xu Dong,Yiwei Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为Aero Hand Open的肌腱驱动仿人手，并开放了其机械、仿真、训练和部署相关资源。其设计便于仿真端到端训练无需实际微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌腱驱动的仿人手在减少成本和电机需求的同时，带来难以模拟和学习的挑战。作者期望推出一款高度可模拟、利于端到端训练的仿人手平台。

Method: 1. 设计并实现了Aero Hand Open这种可以仿真的肌腱驱动仿人手；2. 构建了能够解析肌腱传动关系的仿真模型；3. 建立了电机指令与手部动作映射关系（包括拇指的三向耦合）；4. 提供强化学习包以实现在仿真中训练策略并直接部署到实物手。

Result: 构建了Aero Hand Open的机械方案、仿真模型及映射关系，并证明可以直接在仿真中训练并迁移到实际手部，无需微调或状态估计。

Conclusion: Aero Hand Open作为开放平台，降低了肌腱驱动仿人手的研发门槛，有望加速相关领域的研究与应用。

Abstract: Tendon-driven hands are anthropomorphic, and moving the actuators off the joints is what makes a hand of this capability affordable to build. Two effects produce that saving. Routing force through a cable removes the requirement that a motor fit inside the joint it drives, so smaller and cheaper motors suffice, and one motor can drive several joints through a single cable, so fewer motors are needed. They are also harder to learn on than a direct-drive hand. The underactuated transmission that produces the saving is itself difficult to represent in a simulator, and the joints one cable drives are not independently commandable. We present Aero Hand Open, a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand that is released simulation-ready. Three things ship with it. A simulation model reproduces the cable transmission itself. An identified actuation map connects that model to the motor commands in both directions, including the three-way coupling of the thumb. A reinforcement learning package trains policies for the hand. Together they let a policy be trained entirely in simulation and run on the hand with no fine-tuning and no state estimation. We release the mechanical design, the simulation model, the identified mapping, the training environment and the deployment stack.

</details>
